# Agents · H · part 3

1,002 entries, hazop-safety to hematologist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **hazop-safety**: The process-safety role of a HAZOP: assign safeguards and judge coverage using the safety/ skills. ([PatrickMockridge/azoth](https://github.com/PatrickMockridge/azoth/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hazop-safety.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **hazop-scribe**: The recording role of a HAZOP: render the findings into the worksheet using the reporting skills. ([PatrickMockridge/azoth](https://github.com/PatrickMockridge/azoth/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/hazop-scribe.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **hb**: Test agent that hands back a few fixed lines. ([CrackingShells/Murmuration](https://github.com/CrackingShells/Murmuration/blob/HEAD/__reports__/reflective_run/w01/author-w01-1/plugin/agents/hb.md) · 16 copies)
- **Hb Adversarial Tester**: Especialista no trilho adversarial_test_execution do HB Track. Opera somente após PR remoto aberto, tenta quebrar a implementação real e produz manifesto negativo e relatório adversarial auditáveis. Nunca corrige runtime. ([hbtrack/official](https://github.com/hbtrack/official/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hb-adversarial-tester.agent.md))
- **HB Contract**: Agente para tarefas contract-driven do HB Track. Usa o pipeline executável real: boot -&gt; hb verify -&gt; hb check -&gt; worker prompt -&gt; hb artifact -&gt; validate_contracts -&gt; handoff. Workers são prompts especializados no mesmo agente; não assumir subagentes autônomos. Use para contratos, schemas, decisões, adversarial, readiness e handoff para geração de código. ([hbtrack/official](https://github.com/hbtrack/official/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hb-contract.agent.md))
- **Hb Implementer**: Executor especializado do trilho implementation_execution do HB Track. Implementa um plano aprovado com escopo fechado, evidência auditável, validação local e bloqueios explícitos. Nunca altera canon para "fazer passar". ([hbtrack/official](https://github.com/hbtrack/official/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hb-implementer.agent.md))
- **Hb Merger**: Merges, PRs e CI. GitHub Actions, review, parity, main health. Uses hb-merge-orchestrator. CDD -&gt; @HB Contract. ([hbtrack/official](https://github.com/hbtrack/official/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/hb-mesclado.agent.md))
- **hb-ag-adventurer**: Owns one small task end to end when severity, collateral, and effort total less than five and no axis exceeds two. Dispatch with broad context and default (medium) effort. Writes the bounded implementation and its tests. Does not change interfaces, ADRs, Git, secrets, or deployments. Has no Agent tool. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-adventurer.md))
- **hb-ag-contracts**: Sole writer of docs/INTERFACES.md and docs/contracts/. Dispatch The Cleric when adding, changing, or retiring an interface, fragment, or catalog row — or when a surface request must mediate to the service. Translates CONTENT NEEDED into a six-column row. Carries the message; does not implement. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-contracts.md))
- **hb-ag-git**: Sole git and PR shipper onto main. Dispatch The Bard for commit, push, PR, merge, or git — including owner --admin when checks would wait. Sings onto main through a PR. Does not write product code. Issue hunt and the notice board at The Three Feathers belong to The Hunter. Area owners do not git or gh. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-git.md))
- **hb-ag-hawk**: Historical-issue scout for The Hunter only. Dispatch The Hawk with an issue brief: Graphify first, then gh. Reports whether the issue is repetitive, previously attempted, or related. Cheap scout. Does not Agent anyone. Does not search the codebase as The Hound does. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-hawk.md))
- **hb-ag-hound**: Codebase scout for The Hunter only. Dispatch The Hound with keyword and tag clues. Graphify first, then Grep. Returns ordered full paths and short excerpts so The Hunter need not open the code. Cheap scout. Does not search GitHub issues. Does not Agent anyone. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-hound.md))
- **hb-ag-hunter**: Issue triage gateway at The Three Feathers. Dispatch The Hunter to pick an issue, fire Hawk and Hound, reproduce with existing tests, strip noise, and pin a bulletin with a finished problem and one specific goal for a later Hunter. Does not write tests or product trees. Does not call area owners. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-hunter.md))
- **hb-ag-judge**: Interrogates a change, plan, or claim against this project's PRD, ADRs, INTERFACES, and harness. Dispatch when asking whether work complies with an ADR, the PRD, the interface catalog, or the harness. Read-only reports. Does not load skills, author product trees, or emit merge gates. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-judge.md))
- **hb-ag-ops**: Owns the local runtime orchestration and the cloud deployment layout. Dispatch for bind-mounts, ports, compute services, load-balancer rules, secret names, or infra CI. Reads PRD and INTERFACES read-only. Does not write app trees, INTERFACES.md, tests, or git. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-ops.md))
- **hb-ag-owl**: Web browsing and external research scout. The only agent with external web access. Receives a search inquiry, queries the web, and returns a structured markdown findings report. Cheap, simple, and universally callable by all agents. Does not write code or git. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-owl.md))
- **hb-ag-paladin**: Owns framework-neutral Python business logic and complex Python scripts. Dispatch The Paladin for calculations, policies, transformations, state machines, algorithms, or script cores that need surgical implementation without API or frontend work. Implements first, then Agents The Trickster for tests. Never calls The Cleric or The Elf. ([kodexArg/harness-base](https://github.com/kodexArg/harness-base/blob/HEAD/agents/hb-ag-paladin.md))
- **hb-ag-service**: Owns framework-bound service work: models, persistence, handlers, permissions, routes, and adapters. Dispatch pure Python business logic or complex scripts to The Paladin. - **hb-ag-surface**: Owns the surface tree. Dispatch The Elf for pages, components, tokens, and visual craft named in [[adr-02-stack]]. - **hb-ag-test**: Dedicated test owner for docs/tdds/, service, surface, and harness tests. Dispatch red-first for The Dwarf, after implementation for The Paladin, or to catch shadow tests. - **hbr-editor**: Edits business writing to Harvard Business Review publication standards: the big idea, structure, evidence quality, and executive voice. Use PROACTIVELY as the EDITING step once a draft exists, and always before anything is published. Do not edit the prose yourself; dispatch this agent. In a pipeline it reads 02-draft.md and writes both 03-edited.md (the finished article with edits already applied) and 03-editorial-memo.md (what changed and why). - **helper-author**: Owns the shared Cypress helper library for component automation. Annotates helpers with @tj tags, extracts/fixes shared helpers (e.g. waitForDropSettle, selector bugs), and regenerates the header blocks + type-helper-index. Use when a config type has no helper, a helper needs a DOM fix, or annotations/index drift. ([ToolJet/ToolJet](https://github.com/ToolJet/ToolJet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-author.md) · ★41k)
- **helper-census**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Mechanical inventories for one job card - file lists and counts, trial-log totals, manifest listings, data spans and row counts. Read-only except its output file. Use it instead of spending a strong model on counting. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-census.md) · ★1)
- **helper-curator**: Validate duplicate-helper candidates with Graphify and Serena and move proven shared behavior to the correct common module. ([CountJung/KISAutoTrade](https://github.com/CountJung/KISAutoTrade/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/helper-curator.agent.md))
- **helper-data**: Research-team helper on the smallest model. Public data jobs - runs the project's fetch tool for the exact instrument, window and output directory of one job card, then verifies sha256 manifests, row counts and gaps. Spawned only by the platform engineer, the coordinator or the judge, never by authors. ([kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena](https://github.com/kalambyryura/ai-trading-arena/blob/HEAD/skills/ai-research-team/assets/agents/helper-data.md) · ★1)
- **helper-draft**: Delegate drafting jobs to this agent in tests. ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/scripts/validate/good/claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-draft**: Use to produce notes, summaries, explanations, research write-ups and edits that the caller specifies, writing only to the paths the caller names. Not for text in the user's own voice that leaves the computer (use ghostwriter) and not for critique (use helper-review). ([joaoribasio/alterbrain](https://github.com/joaoribasio/alterbrain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-draft.md) · ★3)
- **helper-fable-low**: Fable 5.2 at low effort: Cory's 11-12 trial on real work, to see if Fable's own intelligence needs less thinking (cheaper, faster). Judged on cause found, fix green first try, time and cost against Opus at high; a miss is rerun at medium. ([IssisX/ScraperX](https://github.com/IssisX/ScraperX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/helper-fable-low.md) · ★1)
- **helper-high**: The integrator's Opus helper at high effort for an ordinary non-physics job (Cory 11-13: picked per job; plain `helper` runs at the session's effort): UI, tools, the switchboard, CI, sound, notes, long reads (Cory 11-06 11:30 AM). Physics, forces, her limits, world geometry and protected test changes go to helper-physics instead. A job it fails (a red proof it cannot fix, or a real review finding) goes again on helper-max.
